    '.hp-chart-wrap{overflow-x:auto;overflow-y:hidden;max-width:100%;min-width:0}' +
    '.hp-chart{width:100%;height:auto;max-height:230px;display:block;overflow:visible}' +
    '@media (max-width:640px){.hp-chart{min-width:520px}.hp-theme{grid-template-columns:1fr auto}.hp-theme-bar{grid-column:1/-1;order:3}}' +
    '.hp-goal-txt{font-weight:800;fill:var(--olive)}' +
    '.hp-bar{fill:color-mix(in srgb,var(--accent) 75%,var(--paper))}.hp-bar-hit{fill:var(--he)}.hp-bar0{fill:var(--line)}.hp-spark-bar{fill:color-mix(in srgb,var(--accent) 75%,var(--paper))}' +
    '.hp-axis{fill:color-mix(in srgb,var(--muted) 70%,var(--ink-soft));font:600 10.5px var(--font-ui)}.hp-val{fill:var(--ink);font:800 11px var(--font-ui)}' +
    '.hp-goal-line{stroke:var(--olive);stroke-width:1.5;stroke-dasharray:5 5}.hp-grid{stroke:var(--line);stroke-width:1}.hp-base{stroke:var(--line-strong);stroke-width:1}' +
    '.hp-line{fill:none;stroke:var(--accent);stroke-width:2;stroke-linejoin:round;stroke-linecap:round}.hp-dot{fill:var(--accent);stroke:var(--paper);stroke-width:2}' +
    '.hp-hit{fill:transparent;cursor:crosshair}.hp-hit:hover{fill:color-mix(in srgb,var(--accent) 8%,transparent)}' +
    '.hp-tip{position:fixed;z-index:10001;pointer-events:none;background:var(--ink);color:var(--paper);border-radius:10px;padding:6px 10px;font:600 12px var(--font-ui);box-shadow:var(--shadow-pop);display:grid;gap:1px;max-width:240px}' +
    '.hp-tip b{font:800 13px var(--font-ui)}.hp-tip span{opacity:.8}.hp-tip[hidden]{display:none}' +
    '.hp-table{margin-top:8px}.hp-table summary{font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--ink-soft);cursor:pointer;width:max-content}' +
    '.hp-table table{border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12.5px;margin-top:6px;max-height:260px}.hp-table caption{text-align:left;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted);padding:2px 0 4px}' +
    '.hp-table th,.hp-table td{padding:3px 12px 3px 0;text-align:left;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}.hp-table td{font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}.hp-table thead th{color:var(--muted)}' +
    '.hp-spark{width:120px;height:30px;display:block}' +
    '.hp-themes{display:grid;gap:9px}.hp-theme{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(110px,190px) 1fr 54px;gap:10px;align-items:center;font-size:13.5px}' +
    '.hp-theme-name{font-weight:600;color:var(--ink-soft);overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap}' +
    '.hp-runs{display:inline-block;margin-left:4px;padding:0 6px;border-radius:8px;background:var(--mint);color:var(--olive);font-size:11px;font-weight:800;vertical-align:1px}' +
    '.hp-theme-bar{position:relative;height:10px;border-radius:99px;background:var(--line);overflow:hidden}' +
    '.hp-theme-bar i{position:absolute;left:0;top:0;bottom:0;border-radius:99px}.hp-theme-bar .seen{background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--olive) 55%,var(--paper))}' +
    '.hp-theme-bar .m{background:var(--olive);box-shadow:2px 0 0 var(--paper)}.hp-theme-bar .solid{background:var(--accent)}.hp-theme-num{text-align:right;font-weight:800;color:var(--ink-soft);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}' +
    '.hp-legend2{display:flex;gap:6px 12px;align-items:center;font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:10px}' +
    '.hp-legend2 i{display:inline-block;width:12px;height:8px;border-radius:9px}.hp-legend2 .m{background:var(--olive)}.hp-legend2 .seen{background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--olive) 55%,var(--paper))}' +
    '.hp-legend2 .hit{background:var(--he)}.hp-legend2 .below{background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--accent) 75%,var(--paper))}' +
    '.hp-badges{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(132px,1fr));gap:10px}' +
    '.hp-badge{display:grid;justify-items:center;text-align:center;gap:3px;padding:14px 8px;border-radius:18px;background:var(--zebra);border:1px dashed var(--line-strong)}.hp-badge:not(.on) .hp-badge-ic{opacity:.5}' +
    '.hp-badge .hp-badge-ic{font-size:28px;filter:grayscale(1)}.hp-badge b{font:800 13px var(--font-display);color:var(--ink-soft)}.hp-badge small{font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);line-height:1.3}' +
    '.hp-badge.on{opacity:1;background:var(--accent-tint);border:1px solid var(--accent-soft)}.hp-badge.on .hp-badge-ic{filter:none}.hp-badge.on b{color:var(--accent)}' +
    '.hp-empty{color:var(--muted);font-style:italic;font-size:14px}' +
    '.hp-kpis{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(118px,1fr));gap:10px}' +
    '.hp-kpi{border-radius:18px;padding:14px 12px;background:var(--accent-tint)}.hp-kpi b{display:block;font:900 24px/1.1 var(--font-display);color:var(--accent)}' +
    '.hp-delta{font:800 11px var(--font-ui);color:var(--ink-soft);vertical-align:middle;white-space:nowrap}.hp-delta i{font-style:normal}.hp-delta.up i{color:var(--olive)}.hp-delta.down i{color:var(--rose)}' +
    '.hp-of{font-size:.55em;color:var(--ink-soft);font-weight:700}' +
    '.hp-kpi span{font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--ink-soft)}.hp-kpi.green{background:var(--mint)}.hp-kpi.green b{color:var(--olive)}' +
    '.hp-kpi.gold{background:var(--peach)}.hp-kpi.gold b{color:var(--gold)}.hp-kpi.rose{background:var(--rose-tint)}.hp-kpi.rose b{color:var(--rose)}' +
    '.hp-kpi.sky{background:var(--sky-tint)}.hp-kpi.sky b{color:var(--sky)}' +
    '.hp-weak{list-style:none;padding:0;margin:0;display:grid;gap:2px}.hp-weak li{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) minmax(0,1fr) minmax(0,1.5fr);align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:8px 4px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);font-size:14px}' +
    '.hp-weak .he{font-family:var(--font-he);font-weight:600;color:var(--he);direction:rtl;text-align:left}.hp-weak small{color:var(--muted);font-weight:600;text-align:right}' +
    '@media(max-width:560px){.hp-weak li{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}.hp-weak small{grid-column:1/-1;white-space:normal}}';

  function injectCss() {
    if (document.getElementById('hp-css')) return;
    var s = document.createElement('style'); s.id = 'hp-css'; s.textContent = CSS;
    document.head.appendChild(s);
    if (document.body) installTip(); else document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', installTip);
  }

  return {
    XP: XP, BADGES: BADGES, levelInfo: levelInfo, dayKey: dayKey, parseDay: parseDay, addDays: addDays,
    shortDate: shortDate, emptyDay: emptyDay, dayActive: dayActive, dayGood: dayGood, dayBad: dayBad,
    streaks: streaks, totalsFrom: totalsFrom, themeMastery: themeMastery, weakWords: weakWords,
    badgeContext: badgeContext, summarize: summarize, weekly: weekly, rangeSum: rangeSum, rate: rate,
    esc: esc, fmt: fmt, levelCard: levelCard, heatmap: heatmap, xpBars: xpBars, rateLine: rateLine,
    themeBars: themeBars, badgesGrid: badgesGrid, sparkline: sparkline, injectCss: injectCss
  };
})();
</script>
<script>
/* =====================================================================
   HPG — gamification branchée sur le site de révision (XP, niveaux,
   séries, badges, toasts) + onglet « Mes stats » enrichi.
   S'appuie sur HP (progress.js) et sur les globales du site :
   storage, saveData, updateWordStat, VOCAB.
   ===================================================================== */
var HPG = (function () {
  'use strict';
  var P, combo = 0, lastLevel = 1, syncTimer = null;

  function prog() {
    if (!storage.progress || typeof storage.progress !== 'object') storage.progress = {};
    P = storage.progress;
    if (typeof P.xp !== 'number') P.xp = 0;
    if (!P.days) P.days = {};
    if (!P.badges) P.badges = {};
    if (!P.goal) P.goal = 60;
    if (typeof P.bestCombo !== 'number') P.bestCombo = 0;
    if (!P.backfilled) {
      /* XP rétroactif : les réponses déjà enregistrées avant la mise en place des points */
      var t = HP.totalsFrom(storage, VOCAB), X = HP.XP;
      P.xp += t.qOk * X.quizOk + t.qKo * X.quizKo + t.fK * X.flashKnown + t.fA * X.flashAgain +
              t.fS * X.flashSoft + t.cK * X.conjKnown + t.cA * X.conjAgain + t.cS * X.conjSoft +
              t.mastered * X.mastered;
      P.backfilled = HP.dayKey();
    }
    return P;
  }

  function today() {
    var k = HP.dayKey();
    return P.days[k] || (P.days[k] = HP.emptyDay());
  }

  /* ---------- toasts & célébrations ---------- */
  function toastBox() {
    var b = document.getElementById('hp-toasts');
    if (!b) { b = document.createElement('div'); b.id = 'hp-toasts'; document.body.appendChild(b); }
    return b;
  }
  function toast(html, kind) {
    var el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'hp-toast ' + (kind || '');
    el.innerHTML = html;
    toastBox().appendChild(el);
    setTimeout(function () { el.classList.add('out'); }, kind === 'xp' ? 900 : 2600);
    setTimeout(function () { el.remove(); }, kind === 'xp' ? 1300 : 3000);
  }
  function celebrate(title, sub, big) {
    var ov = document.createElement('div');
    ov.className = 'hp-cele';
    ov.innerHTML = '<div class="hp-cele-card"><div class="hp-cele-big">' + big + '</div><h3>' + title + '</h3><p>' + sub +
      '</p><button type="button" class="btn primary">Continuer</button></div>';
    ov.addEventListener('click', function (e) { if (e.target === ov || e.target.tagName === 'BUTTON') ov.remove(); });
    document.body.appendChild(ov);
    var conf = '';
    for (var i = 0; i < 28; i++) {
      conf += '<i style="left:' + (Math.random() * 100).toFixed(1) + '%;animation-delay:' + (Math.random() * .5).toFixed(2) +
        's;background:var(' + ['--accent', '--accent-2', '--olive', '--gold', '--rose', '--sky'][i % 6] + ')"></i>';
    }
    ov.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<div class="hp-confetti">' + conf + '</div>');
  }

  /* ---------- gain d'XP ---------- */
  /* objectif du jour : vérifié après chaque ajout d'XP (réponse ou bonus de série) */
  function checkGoal(d) {
    if (!d.goal && d.xp >= P.goal) {
      d.goal = 1; d.xp += HP.XP.goal; P.xp += HP.XP.goal;
      toast('🎯 <b>Objectif du jour atteint</b> · +' + HP.XP.goal + ' XP bonus', 'goal');
    }
  }
  function gain(n, fields) {
    prog();
    var d = today();
    for (var f in fields) d[f] = (d[f] || 0) + fields[f];
    d.xp += n; P.xp += n;
    var h = new Date().getHours();
    if (h < 8) P.early = true;
    if (h >= 22) P.night = true;
    checkGoal(d);
    toast('+' + n + ' XP', 'xp');
    var L = HP.levelInfo(P.xp);
    if (L.level > lastLevel) {
      celebrate('Niveau ' + L.level + ' !', L.title + ' · <span class="hp-he">' + L.titleHe + '</span> — continue comme ça.',
        '<span class="hp-he">' + L.letter + '</span>');
    }
    lastLevel = L.level;
    checkBadges();
    hud();
  }

  function onAnswer(ok) {
    if (ok) {
      combo++;
      if (combo > P.bestCombo) P.bestCombo = combo;
      if (combo % 10 === 0) {
        var d = today();
        P.xp += HP.XP.combo; d.xp += HP.XP.combo;
        toast('⚡ <b>' + combo + ' bonnes réponses d\'affilée</b> · +' + HP.XP.combo + ' XP', 'goal');
        checkGoal(d);
      }
    } else combo = 0;
  }

  function masteredNow(k) {
    var q = storage.wordStats && storage.wordStats[k], f = storage.flash && storage.flash[k];
    var qm = q && ((q.correct >= 3 && q.wrong === 0) || (q.seen >= 4 && q.correct / q.seen >= 0.8));
    var fm = f && ((f.known >= 2 && !f.again && !f.soft) || (f.seen >= 4 && f.known / f.seen >= 0.8));
    return !!(qm || fm);
  }

  function checkBadges() {
    var totals = HP.totalsFrom(storage, VOCAB), themes = HP.themeMastery(storage, VOCAB);
    var c = HP.badgeContext(P, totals, themes);
    HP.BADGES.forEach(function (b) {
      if (!P.badges[b.id] && b.test(c)) {
        P.badges[b.id] = HP.dayKey();
        toast('<span style="font-size:22px">' + b.icon + '</span> <span><b>Badge débloqué</b><br>' + HP.esc(b.name) + '</span>', 'badge');
      }
    });
  }

  /* ---------- crochets appelés par le site ---------- */
  function before(e) {
    if (!e || e.conj) return false;
    return masteredNow(e[0] + '|' + e[1]);
  }
  function onFlash(e, verdict, wasMastered) {
    prog();
    var conj = e && e.conj, X = HP.XP, n, f = {};
    if (conj) {
      n = verdict === 'known' ? X.conjKnown : verdict === 'soft' ? X.conjSoft : X.conjAgain;
      f[verdict === 'known' ? 'cK' : verdict === 'soft' ? 'cS' : 'cA'] = 1;
    } else {
      n = verdict === 'known' ? X.flashKnown : verdict === 'soft' ? X.flashSoft : X.flashAgain;
      f[verdict === 'known' ? 'fK' : verdict === 'soft' ? 'fS' : 'fA'] = 1;
      if (!wasMastered && masteredNow(e[0] + '|' + e[1])) { n += X.mastered; toast('📗 Mot maîtrisé : <span class="hp-he">' + HP.esc(e[0]) + '</span>', 'goal'); }
    }
    onAnswer(verdict === 'known');
    gain(n, f);
  }
  function onQuiz(item, result, wasMastered) {
    prog();
    var ok = result === 'correct', n = ok ? HP.XP.quizOk : HP.XP.quizKo;
    if (ok && !wasMastered && masteredNow(item.he + '|' + item.fr)) { n += HP.XP.mastered; toast('📗 Mot maîtrisé : <span class="hp-he">' + HP.esc(item.he) + '</span>', 'goal'); }
    onAnswer(ok);
    gain(n, ok ? { qOk: 1 } : { qKo: 1 });
  }

  /* ---------- synchronisation vers l'espace de la prof ---------- */
  function send() {
    try {
      if (window.parent && window.parent !== window && window.__HP_STUDENT__) {
        window.parent.postMessage({ hp: 'progress', summary: HP.summarize(storage, prog(), VOCAB), storage: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(storage)) }, '*');
      }
    } catch (e) { /* hors espace élève */ }
  }
  function sync() {
    clearTimeout(syncTimer);
    syncTimer = setTimeout(function () { syncTimer = null; send(); }, 1200);
  }
  /* envoi immédiat de ce qui attend encore (déconnexion, page fermée) */
  function flush() { if (syncTimer) { clearTimeout(syncTimer); syncTimer = null; send(); } }
  window.addEventListener('pagehide', flush);
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () { if (document.visibilityState === 'hidden') flush(); });

  /* ---------- bandeau en-tête ---------- */
  function hud() {
    var el = document.getElementById('hp-hud');
    if (!el) return;
    var L = HP.levelInfo(P.xp), st = HP.streaks(P.days), d = P.days[HP.dayKey()] || HP.emptyDay();
    el.innerHTML =
      '<button type="button" class="hp-hud-lv" data-go="stats" title="Voir ma progression"><span class="hp-he">' + L.letter + '</span>Niv. ' + L.level +
        '<i><b style="width:' + L.pct + '%"></b></i></button>' +
      '<span class="hp-hud-pill" title="XP total">⭐ <b>' + HP.fmt(P.xp) + '</b> XP</span>' +
      '<span class="hp-hud-pill" title="Jours de révision d\'affilée">' + (st.cur ? '🔥' : '·') + ' <b>' + st.cur + '</b> j</span>' +
      '<span class="hp-hud-pill' + (d.xp >= P.goal ? ' done' : '') + '" title="Objectif du jour">🎯 <b>' + Math.min(d.xp, P.goal) + '</b>/' + P.goal + '</span>';
  }

  /* ---------- onglet Mes stats ---------- */
  function kpi(cls, val, lbl) { return '<div class="hp-kpi ' + cls + '"><b>' + val + '</b><span>' + lbl + '</span></div>'; }
  function delta(a, b) {
    if (a == null || b == null) return '';
    var d = a - b; if (!d) return ' <small class="hp-delta" aria-label="stable">=</small>';
    return ' <small class="hp-delta ' + (d > 0 ? 'up' : 'down') + '" aria-label="' + (d > 0 ? '+' : '−') + Math.abs(d) + ' par rapport à la semaine précédente"><i aria-hidden="true">' + (d > 0 ? '▲' : '▼') + '</i>' + Math.abs(d) + '</small>';
  }
  function renderStats() {
    var root = document.getElementById('hp-progress');
    if (!root) return;
    prog();
    var now = new Date(), totals = HP.totalsFrom(storage, VOCAB), themes = HP.themeMastery(storage, VOCAB);
    var w0 = HP.rangeSum(P.days, HP.addDays(now, -6), 7), w1 = HP.rangeSum(P.days, HP.addDays(now, -13), 7);
    var r0 = HP.rate(w0), r1 = HP.rate(w1), active30 = 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < 30; i++) if (HP.dayActive(P.days[HP.dayKey(HP.addDays(now, -i))])) active30++;
    var nWords = themes.reduce(function (s, t) { return s + t.n; }, 0);
    var answers = totals.qOk + totals.qKo + totals.fK + totals.fA + totals.fS + totals.cK + totals.cA + totals.cS;
    var nb = Object.keys(P.badges).length;
    root.innerHTML =
      '<div class="stats-card">' + HP.levelCard(P, { goalPicker: true }) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="stats-card"><h3>Ces 7 derniers jours</h3><div class="hp-kpis">' +
        kpi('', HP.fmt(w0.xp) + delta(w0.xp, w1.xp), 'XP gagnés') +
        kpi('sky', HP.fmt(HP.dayGood(w0) + HP.dayBad(w0)), 'réponses') +
        kpi('green', (r0 == null ? '—' : r0 + '%') + delta(r0, r1), 'de réussite') +
        kpi('gold', active30 + '/30', 'jours actifs (30 j)') +
        kpi('green', HP.fmt(totals.mastered) + '<small class="hp-of">/' + HP.fmt(nWords) + '</small>', 'mots maîtrisés') +
        kpi('rose', HP.fmt(answers), 'réponses au total') +
      '</div><p class="hp-note">Flèches : comparaison avec les 7 jours précédents. Un mot compte comme maîtrisé s\'il l\'est au quiz <em>ou</em> en flashcards.</p></div>' +
      '<div class="stats-card"><h3>Régularité</h3>' + HP.heatmap(P.days, 17) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="stats-card"><h3>XP par jour — 30 derniers jours</h3>' + HP.xpBars(P.days, 30, P.goal) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="stats-card"><h3>Taux de réussite par semaine</h3>' + HP.rateLine(P.days, 8) +
        '<p class="hp-note">Toutes réponses confondues : quiz, flashcards et conjugaison.</p></div>' +
      '<div class="stats-card"><h3>Maîtrise par thème</h3>' + HP.themeBars(themes) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="stats-card"><h3>Badges <small class="hp-of">' + nb + ' / ' + HP.BADGES.length + '</small></h3>' + HP.badgesGrid(P.badges) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="stats-card hp-rules"><h3>Comment gagner des XP</h3><ul>' +
        '<li>Quiz : <b>+' + HP.XP.quizOk + '</b> bonne réponse, <b>+' + HP.XP.quizKo + '</b> erreur (l\'effort compte)</li>' +
        '<li>Flashcard : <b>+' + HP.XP.flashKnown + '</b> « je sais », <b>+' + HP.XP.flashAgain + '</b> « à revoir »</li>' +
        '<li>Conjugaison : <b>+' + HP.XP.conjKnown + '</b> réussie, <b>+' + HP.XP.conjAgain + '</b> à revoir</li>' +
        '<li>Nouveau mot maîtrisé : <b>+' + HP.XP.mastered + '</b> · Objectif du jour : <b>+' + HP.XP.goal + '</b> · 10 bonnes réponses d\'affilée : <b>+' + HP.XP.combo + '</b></li>' +
      '</ul></div>';
    root.querySelectorAll('[data-goal]').forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener('click', function () { P.goal = +b.dataset.goal; saveData(); renderStats(); hud(); });
    });
  }

  var CSS = '' +
    '#hp-hud{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:center;gap:8px;margin:-6px auto 18px}' +
    '.hp-hud-pill,.hp-hud-lv{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;background:var(--glass);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:999px;padding:6px 14px;font-size:13px;color:var(--ink-soft);backdrop-filter:blur(8px)}' +
    '.hp-hud-pill b{color:var(--accent);font-weight:800}.hp-hud-pill.done{background:var(--mint);border-color:transparent}.hp-hud-pill.done b{color:var(--olive)}' +
    '.hp-hud-lv{cursor:pointer;font:800 13px var(--font-ui);color:var(--ink)}.hp-hud-lv .hp-he{display:grid;place-items:center;width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:8px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--accent),var(--accent-2));color:var(--on-accent);font-size:13px}' +
    '.hp-hud-lv i{display:block;width:56px;height:6px;border-radius:9px;background:var(--accent-tint);overflow:hidden}.hp-hud-lv i b{display:block;height:100%;background:var(--accent);border-radius:9px}' +
    '#hp-toasts{position:fixed;right:16px;bottom:16px;z-index:9999;display:grid;gap:8px;justify-items:end;pointer-events:none}' +
    '.hp-toast{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;background:var(--paper);color:var(--ink);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:16px;padding:10px 14px;box-shadow:var(--shadow-pop);font-size:13.5px;animation:hpIn .25s ease-out;transition:opacity .35s,transform .35s}' +
    '.hp-toast.xp{background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--accent),var(--accent-2));color:var(--on-accent);font:900 15px var(--font-display);border:0;padding:7px 14px}' +
    '.hp-toast.goal{background:var(--mint);border-color:transparent}.hp-toast.badge{background:var(--peach);border-color:transparent}' +
    '.hp-toast.out{opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px)}@keyframes hpIn{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(10px) scale(.96)}}' +
    '.hp-cele{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:10000;background:var(--overlay);display:grid;place-items:center;padding:16px;overflow:hidden}' +
    '.hp-cele-card{position:relative;z-index:1;background:var(--paper);border-radius:28px;padding:30px 34px;text-align:center;max-width:360px;box-shadow:var(--shadow-pop);animation:hpPop .4s cubic-bezier(.2,1.4,.4,1)}' +
    '.hp-cele-big{width:96px;height:96px;margin:0 auto 14px;border-radius:30px;display:grid;place-items:center;font:700 52px var(--font-he);color:var(--on-accent);background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--accent),var(--accent-2));transform:rotate(-6deg)}' +
    '.hp-cele-card h3{font:900 28px var(--font-display);margin-bottom:6px}.hp-cele-card p{color:var(--ink-soft);margin-bottom:18px}' +
    '@keyframes hpPop{from{transform:scale(.7);opacity:0}}' +
    '.hp-confetti i{position:absolute;top:-12px;width:9px;height:14px;border-radius:3px;animation:hpFall 1.8s ease-in forwards}' +
    '@keyframes hpFall{to{transform:translateY(105vh) rotate(540deg);opacity:.6}}' +
    '#hp-progress{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr);gap:18px;margin-bottom:18px}#hp-progress .stats-card{margin:0;min-width:0}' +
    '.hp-note{font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);font-style:italic;margin-top:12px}' +
    '.hp-rules ul{margin:0;padding-left:18px;display:grid;gap:6px;font-size:14px;color:var(--ink-soft)}.hp-rules b{color:var(--accent)}' +
    '.hp-sep{font:800 13px var(--font-ui);color:var(--muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;text-align:center;margin:8px 0 14px}' +
    '@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.hp-toast,.hp-cele-card,.hp-confetti i{animation:none}}';

  function install() {
    HP.injectCss();
    var s = document.createElement('style'); s.textContent = CSS; document.head.appendChild(s);
    prog();
    lastLevel = HP.levelInfo(P.xp).level;
    checkBadges();

    /* bandeau sous les compteurs */
    var gs = document.getElementById('global-stats');
    if (gs) {
      var h = document.createElement('div'); h.id = 'hp-hud';
      gs.parentNode.insertBefore(h, gs.nextSibling);
      h.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
        if (e.target.closest('[data-go]')) { var t = document.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="stats"]'); if (t) t.click(); }
      });
    }
    /* onglet stats : la progression passe en tête */
    var panel = document.getElementById('panel-stats');
    if (panel) {
      var root = document.createElement('div'); root.id = 'hp-progress';
      panel.insertBefore(root, panel.firstChild);
      var sep = document.createElement('div'); sep.className = 'hp-sep'; sep.textContent = 'Détail par activité';
      panel.insertBefore(sep, root.nextSibling);
    }
    var t = document.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="stats"]');
    if (t) {
      t.textContent = 'Ma progression';
      t.addEventListener('click', renderStats);
    }

    /* crochet quiz : updateWordStat est une fonction globale, on l'enveloppe */
    var orig = window.updateWordStat;
    if (typeof orig === 'function') {
      updateWordStat = function (item, result) {
        var was = masteredNow(item.he + '|' + item.fr);
        orig(item, result);
        onQuiz(item, result, was);
        saveData();
      };
    }
    /* crochet sauvegarde : synchronise le résumé vers l'espace de la prof */
    var origSave = window.saveData;
    if (typeof origSave === 'function') {
      saveData = function () { origSave(); sync(); };
    }
    saveData();
    hud();
    renderStats();
  }

  return { install: install, flush: flush, before: before, onFlash: onFlash, renderStats: renderStats, hud: hud };
})();

try { HPG.install(); } catch (e) { console.error("HPG", e); }
</script>
<!-- HP:END -->
<script>
/* =====================================================================
   RÉVISER — révision espacée, séance éclair et jeux courts
   (paires express, mot en désordre, conjugaison mélangée).
   storage.srs   : { "he|fr": { b: boîte 0-5, d: "AAAA-MM-JJ" prochaine révision, t: dernier jour de promotion } }
   storage.games : { pairs|scramble|conj : { best, plays, last } }
   Toutes les réponses (quiz, flashcards, jeux) font avancer les mots dans les boîtes.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var root = document.getElementById('rv-root');
  if (!root || typeof VOCAB === 'undefined' || typeof storage === 'undefined') return;
  var tabEl = document.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="revise"]');
  var IV = [1, 1, 3, 7, 14, 30];            /* jours avant la prochaine révision selon la boîte atteinte */
  var BOX = ['à revoir demain', '1 jour', '3 jours', '1 semaine', '2 semaines', '1 mois'];
  var NEW_PER_DAY = 10, FLASH_N = 5, DAY_MAX = 20;
  var NIK = /[֑-ׇ]/g;

  function pad(n) { return (n < 10 ? '0' : '') + n; }
  function dk(d) { d = d || new Date(); return d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(d.getDate()); }
  function addDays(key, n) { var p = key.split('-'); return dk(new Date(+p[0], +p[1] - 1, +p[2] + n)); }
  function esc(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' }[c]; }); }
  function shuffle(a) { a = a.slice(); for (var i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) { var j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1)); var t = a[i]; a[i] = a[j]; a[j] = t; } return a; }
  function plural(n, one, many) { return n + ' ' + (n > 1 ? (many || one + 's') : one); }
  function excl(k) { return Array.isArray(storage.excluded) && storage.excluded.indexOf(k) >= 0; }
  function S() { if (!storage.srs || typeof storage.srs !== 'object') storage.srs = {}; return storage.srs; }
  function save() { if (typeof saveData === 'function') saveData(); }

  /* tous les mots, sans doublon, thèmes de base d'abord */
  function words() {
    var seen = {}, out = [], order = [];
    var core = (typeof CORE_THEMES !== 'undefined' && CORE_THEMES) || [];
    core.forEach(function (t) { if (VOCAB[t] && order.indexOf(t) < 0) order.push(t); });
    Object.keys(VOCAB).forEach(function (t) { if (order.indexOf(t) < 0) order.push(t); });
    order.forEach(function (t) {
      (VOCAB[t] || []).forEach(function (e) {
        if (!e || !e[0] || !e[1]) return;
        var k = e[0] + '|' + e[1];
        if (seen[k] || excl(k)) return;
        seen[k] = 1;
        out.push({ k: k, e: e, he: e[0], fr: e[1], voc: e[2] || e[0], theme: t });
      });
    });
    return out;
  }

  /* première fois : les mots déjà travaillés sont rangés d'après l'historique, étalés sur les jours suivants */
  function bootstrap() {
    if (storage.srsInit) return;
    var srs = S(), t = dk();
    words().forEach(function (w) {
      if (srs[w.k]) return;
      var q = storage.wordStats && storage.wordStats[w.k], f = storage.flash && storage.flash[w.k];
      if (!q && !f) return;
      var ok = (q ? q.correct || 0 : 0) + (f ? f.known || 0 : 0), ko = (q ? q.wrong || 0 : 0) + (f ? f.again || 0 : 0);
      var b = Math.max(0, Math.min(4, ok - ko));
      srs[w.k] = { b: b, d: addDays(t, Math.floor(Math.random() * (b ? IV[b] + 1 : 3))) };
    });
    storage.srsInit = t;
    save();
  }

  /* une réponse : 'ok' fait monter d'une boîte (une fois par jour), 'ko' renvoie au lendemain */
  function answer(k, res) {
    if (!k) return;
    var srs = S(), t = dk(), c = srs[k], isNew = !c;
    c = c || { b: 0, d: t };
    if (res === 'ok') {
      if (c.t !== t) { c.b = isNew ? 1 : Math.min(5, c.b + 1); c.t = t; }
      c.d = addDays(t, IV[c.b]);
    } else if (res === 'soft') {
      c.b = Math.min(c.b, 1); c.d = addDays(t, 3);
    } else {
      c.b = 0; c.d = addDays(t, 1);
    }
    srs[k] = c;
  }

  function due(list) {
    var t = dk(), srs = S();
    return (list || words()).filter(function (w) { return srs[w.k] && srs[w.k].d <= t; }).sort(function (a, b) {
      var x = srs[a.k], y = srs[b.k];
      return x.d < y.d ? -1 : x.d > y.d ? 1 : x.b - y.b;
    });
  }
  function fresh(list) { var srs = S(); return (list || words()).filter(function (w) { return !srs[w.k]; }); }
  function newLeft() {
    var n = storage.srsNew && storage.srsNew.d === dk() ? storage.srsNew.n : 0;
    return Math.max(0, NEW_PER_DAY - n);
  }
  function takeNew(n) {
    if (!storage.srsNew || storage.srsNew.d !== dk()) storage.srsNew = { d: dk(), n: 0 };
    storage.srsNew.n += n;
  }
  function plan(n) {
    var all = words(), d = due(all).slice(0, n), nw = [];
    if (d.length < n) nw = fresh(all).slice(0, Math.min(n - d.length, newLeft()));
    return { due: d, fresh: nw, all: d.concat(nw) };
  }
  function startSession(n, title) {
    var p = plan(n);
    if (!p.all.length || typeof window.openFlash !== 'function') return;
    if (p.fresh.length) { takeNew(p.fresh.length); save(); }
    window.openFlash(title, p.all.map(function (w) { return w.e; }));
  }

  /* ---------- branchements : quiz et flashcards comptent aussi ---------- */
  var origWS = window.updateWordStat;
  if (typeof origWS === 'function') {
    updateWordStat = function (item, result) {
      origWS(item, result);
      if (item && (result === 'correct' || result === 'wrong')) { answer(item.he + '|' + item.fr, result === 'correct' ? 'ok' : 'ko'); save(); }
    };
  }
  if (window.HPG && typeof HPG.onFlash === 'function') {
    var origFlash = HPG.onFlash;
    HPG.onFlash = function (e, verdict, was) {
      origFlash(e, verdict, was);
      if (e && !e.conj && e[0]) answer(e[0] + '|' + e[1], verdict === 'known' ? 'ok' : verdict === 'soft' ? 'soft' : 'ko');
    };
  }
  /* réponse donnée dans un jeu : enregistrée comme une réponse de quiz (XP, statistiques, boîtes) */
  function stat(w, ok) { if (typeof updateWordStat === 'function') updateWordStat({ he: w.he, fr: w.fr }, ok ? 'correct' : 'wrong'); }

  /* ---------- onglet ---------- */
  function badge(n) {
    if (!tabEl) return;
    var b = tabEl.querySelector('.rv-badge');
    if (n > 0) {
      if (!b) { b = document.createElement('span'); b.className = 'rv-badge'; tabEl.appendChild(b); }
      b.textContent = n > 99 ? '99+' : n;
      b.setAttribute('aria-label', plural(n, 'mot') + ' à revoir');
    } else if (b) b.remove();
  }
  function hasConj() { return typeof CONJ !== 'undefined' && Array.isArray(CONJ) && CONJ.filter(okVerb).length >= 2; }
  function okVerb(v) { return v && v.i && Array.isArray(v.p) && Array.isArray(v.a) && Array.isArray(v.u) && v.a.length >= 5 && v.u.length >= 5 && v.p.length >= 3; }

  function render() {
    bootstrap();
    var all = words();
    if (!all.length) {
      root.innerHTML = '<section class="rv-today"><h2>Réviser</h2><p class="rv-sub">Tes mots apparaîtront ici dès que ton vocabulaire sera rempli.</p></section>';
      badge(0); return;
    }
    var srs = S(), t = dk(), d = due(all), fr = fresh(all), nl = Math.min(newLeft(), fr.length);
    var boxes = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0];
    all.forEach(function (w) { var c = srs[w.k]; if (c) boxes[Math.max(0, Math.min(5, c.b))]++; });
    var max = Math.max.apply(null, boxes.concat([1]));
    var tom = 0, week = 0;
    all.forEach(function (w) { var c = srs[w.k]; if (!c || c.d <= t) return; if (c.d === addDays(t, 1)) tom++; if (c.d <= addDays(t, 7)) week++; });
    var dayN = Math.min(DAY_MAX, d.length + (d.length < DAY_MAX ? Math.min(DAY_MAX - d.length, nl) : 0));
    var flashN = Math.min(FLASH_N, d.length + Math.min(nl, FLASH_N));
    var G = storage.games || {};
    if (G.pairs && !G['pairs-60']) { G['pairs-60'] = G.pairs; delete G.pairs; }
    var pd = storage.preferences.pairsDur || 60, pdl = pd >= 60 ? (pd / 60) + ' min' : pd + ' s';
    var rec = function (id, unit, many, sfx) { var g = G[id]; return g && g.plays ? 'Record : ' + plural(g.best, unit, many) + (sfx || '') : 'Pas encore joué'; };

    root.innerHTML =
      '<section class="rv-today">' +
        '<div class="rv-head"><div>' +
          '<h2>Révision du jour</h2>' +
          '<p class="rv-sub">' + (d.length ? '<b>' + plural(d.length, 'mot') + '</b> à revoir aujourd\'hui.' : 'Tout est à jour pour aujourd\'hui.') +
            (nl ? ' ' + plural(nl, 'nouveau mot', 'nouveaux mots') + ' à découvrir.' : '') + '</p>' +
        '</div><div class="rv-big"><b>' + d.length + '</b><span>à revoir</span></div></div>' +
        '<div class="rv-acts">' +
          '<button type="button" class="btn primary" data-rv="flash"' + (flashN ? '' : ' disabled') + '>Séance éclair · ' + plural(flashN || FLASH_N, 'mot') + '</button>' +
          '<button type="button" class="btn" data-rv="day"' + (dayN ? '' : ' disabled') + '>' +
            (dayN ? 'Révision du jour · ' + plural(dayN, 'mot') : 'Rien d\'autre à revoir aujourd\'hui') + '</button>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div class="rv-boxes" role="img" aria-label="Répartition de tes mots par délai de révision">' + boxes.map(function (n, i) {
          return '<div class="rv-box"><i><b style="height:' + Math.round(100 * n / max) + '%"></b></i><strong>' + n + '</strong>' + BOX[i] + '</div>';
        }).join('') + '</div>' +
        '<p class="rv-note">Un mot réussi revient de plus en plus tard : 1, 3, 7, 14 puis 30 jours. Un mot raté revient le lendemain. ' +
          'Tes réponses au quiz, aux flashcards et aux jeux comptent aussi.' +
          (tom || week ? ' Demain : ' + plural(tom, 'mot') + ' · 7 prochains jours : ' + plural(week, 'mot') + '.' : '') + '</p>' +
      '</section>' +
      '<h3 class="rv-h">Jeux courts</h3>' +
      '<div class="rv-games">' +
        '<button type="button" class="rv-game" data-rv="pairs"><span class="rv-ic" aria-hidden="true">🔗</span><b>Paires express</b>' +
          '<span>Relie le plus de paires hébreu–français possible avant la fin du temps : 1, 2, 3 ou 5 minutes.</span><small>' + rec('pairs-' + pd, 'paire', null, ' en ' + pdl) + '</small></button>' +
        '<button type="button" class="rv-game" data-rv="scramble"><span class="rv-ic" aria-hidden="true">🔤</span><b>Mot en désordre</b>' +
          '<span>Remets les lettres dans l\'ordre, de droite à gauche. 10 mots.</span><small>' + rec('scramble', 'mot') + '</small></button>' +
        (hasConj() ? '<button type="button" class="rv-game" data-rv="conj"><span class="rv-ic" aria-hidden="true">🔁</span><b>Conjugaison mélangée</b>' +
          '<span>Choisis la bonne forme pour la personne et le temps demandés. 12 questions.</span><small>' + rec('conj', 'bonne réponse', 'bonnes réponses') + '</small></button>' : '') +
      '</div>';
    badge(d.length);
  }

  root.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target.closest('[data-rv]');
    if (!b || b.disabled) return;
    var a = b.dataset.rv;
    if (a === 'flash') return startSession(FLASH_N, 'Séance éclair');
    if (a === 'day') return startSession(DAY_MAX, 'Révision du jour');
    if (GAMES[a]) return openGame(a);
  });

  /* ---------- fenêtre des jeux (même style que les flashcards) ---------- */
  var ov, gBody, gTitle, gProg, gTimer, gBar, game = null;
  function ensureOv() {
    if (ov) return;
    ov = document.createElement('div');
    ov.className = 'flash-ov rv-ov'; ov.id = 'rv-ov';
    ov.setAttribute('role', 'dialog'); ov.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true');
    ov.innerHTML = '<div class="flash-box"><div class="flash-top"><span class="flash-title" id="rv-title"></span>' +
      '<button type="button" class="flash-close" data-rvclose="1" title="Fermer (Échap)" aria-label="Fermer">&times;</button></div>' +
      '<div class="flash-sub"><span id="rv-prog"></span><span class="rv-timer" id="rv-timer"></span></div>' +
      '<div id="rv-body"></div><div class="flash-bar"><i id="rv-bar" style="width:0"></i></div></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(ov);
    gBody = ov.querySelector('#rv-body'); gTitle = ov.querySelector('#rv-title'); gProg = ov.querySelector('#rv-prog');
    gTimer = ov.querySelector('#rv-timer'); gBar = ov.querySelector('#rv-bar');
    ov.addEventListener('click', onClick);
  }
  function xpNow() { return (storage.progress && storage.progress.xp) || 0; }
  function openGame(id, opt) {
    ensureOv();
    stop();
    game = { id: id, opt: opt, xp0: xpNow() };
    gTitle.textContent = GAMES[id].title; gTimer.textContent = ''; gTimer.classList.remove('low'); gBar.style.width = '0';
    document.body.classList.add('rv-quiet');           /* pas de « +10 XP » à chaque réponse pendant un jeu rapide */
    ov.classList.add('on'); document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    GAMES[id].start(opt);
  }
  function stop() { if (game && game.timer) { clearInterval(game.timer); game.timer = null; } if (game && game.wait) { clearTimeout(game.wait); game.wait = null; } }
  function closeGame() {
    stop(); game = null;
    if (ov) ov.classList.remove('on');
    document.body.classList.remove('rv-quiet'); document.body.style.overflow = '';
    if (window.HPG && HPG.hud) { try { HPG.hud(); } catch (x) {} }
    render();
  }
  function record(id, score) {
    if (!storage.games || typeof storage.games !== 'object') storage.games = {};
    var g = storage.games[id] || (storage.games[id] = { best: 0, plays: 0 });
    var prev = g.plays ? g.best : -1;
    g.plays++; g.last = dk();
    if (score > g.best) g.best = score;
    save();
    return { record: score > prev && prev >= 0 && score > 0, first: prev < 0, best: g.best };
  }
  function endScreen(id, score, big, msg, unit, extra) {
    stop();
    var r = record(id, score), gained = xpNow() - game.xp0;
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.hp-toast.xp'), function (t) { t.remove(); });
    document.body.classList.remove('rv-quiet');
    gBar.style.width = '100%'; gProg.textContent = 'Terminé'; gTimer.textContent = '';
    gBody.innerHTML = '<div class="flash-end"><div class="big">' + big + '</div><p>' + msg + (gained > 0 ? ' · +' + gained + ' XP' : '') + '</p>' +
      (r.record ? '<p class="rv-best">Nouveau record !</p>' : (!r.first ? '<p class="rv-sub">Record : ' + plural(r.best, unit[0], unit[1]) + '</p>' : '')) +
      '<div class="flash-acts"><button class="btn primary" data-rvagain="1">Rejouer</button>' + (extra || '') + '<button class="btn" data-rvclose="1">Fermer</button></div></div>';
    var b = gBody.querySelector('[data-rvagain]'); if (b) try { b.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (x) {}
  }
  function onClick(e) {
    if (e.target === ov || e.target.closest('[data-rvclose]')) return closeGame();
    if (e.target.closest('[data-rvagain]')) return openGame(game.id, game.opt);
    if (e.target.closest('[data-rvchoose]')) return openGame(game.id);
    if (game && GAMES[game.id].click) GAMES[game.id].click(e);
  }
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (!ov || !ov.classList.contains('on')) return;
    if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); return closeGame(); }
    if (e.key === 'Enter') {
      var nx = gBody.querySelector('[data-rvnext]') || gBody.querySelector('[data-rvagain]');
      if (nx) { e.preventDefault(); nx.click(); }
      return;
    }
    if (game && GAMES[game.id].key) GAMES[game.id].key(e);
  });

  /* mots pour les jeux : ceux déjà rencontrés d'abord (les plus fragiles en tête), puis les autres */
  function pick(n, filter, avoid) {
    var srs = S(), t = dk();
    var all = words().filter(function (w) { return (!filter || filter(w)) && !(avoid && avoid[w.k]); });
    var known = all.filter(function (w) { return srs[w.k]; }).map(function (w) {
      var c = srs[w.k]; return { w: w, s: (c.d <= t ? 0 : 6) + c.b + Math.random() * 4 };
    }).sort(function (a, b) { return a.s - b.s; }).map(function (x) { return x.w; });
    var out = known.slice(0, n);
    if (out.length < n) out = out.concat(shuffle(all.filter(function (w) { return !srs[w.k]; })).slice(0, n - out.length));
    return shuffle(out);
  }
  function plain(he) { return String(he || '').replace(NIK, ''); }

  var GAMES = {
    /* ---------- Paires express : 60 secondes ---------- */
    pairs: {
      title: 'Paires express',
      DURS: [[60, '1 min'], [120, '2 min'], [180, '3 min'], [300, '5 min']],
      start: function (sec) {
        if (!sec) return this.choose();
        storage.preferences.pairsDur = sec; save();
        game.total = sec; game.score = 0; game.err = 0; game.left = sec; game.used = {}; game.sel = null;
        this.round();
        var self = this;
        game.timer = setInterval(function () {
          game.left--;
          self.clock();
          if (game.left <= 0) self.end();
        }, 1000);
        this.clock();
      },
      /* écran de départ : durée de la partie (chaque durée a son record) */
      choose: function () {
        var G = storage.games || {}, pref = storage.preferences.pairsDur || 60;
        gProg.textContent = 'Choisis la durée'; gTimer.textContent = ''; gBar.style.width = '0';
        gBody.innerHTML = '<p class="rv-help">Combien de temps veux-tu jouer ?</p><div class="rv-durs">' + this.DURS.map(function (d, i) {
          var g = G['pairs-' + d[0]];
          return '<button type="button" class="rv-dur' + (d[0] === pref ? ' pref' : '') + '" data-dur="' + d[0] + '"><b>' + d[1] + '</b>' +
            '<small>' + (g && g.plays ? 'record : ' + plural(g.best, 'paire') : 'pas encore joué') + '</small><span class="rv-num" aria-hidden="true">' + (i + 1) + '</span></button>';
        }).join('') + '</div>';
        var b = gBody.querySelector('.rv-dur.pref'); if (b) try { b.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (x) {}
      },
      fmt: function (n) { return n >= 60 ? Math.floor(n / 60) + ':' + pad(n % 60) : n + ' s'; },
      key: function (e) {
        if (game.total || !/^[1-4]$/.test(e.key)) return;
        e.preventDefault(); game.opt = this.DURS[+e.key - 1][0]; this.start(game.opt);
      },
      clock: function () {
        gTimer.textContent = this.fmt(game.left);
        gTimer.classList.toggle('low', game.left <= 10);
        gBar.style.width = Math.round(100 * (game.total - game.left) / game.total) + '%';
        gProg.textContent = plural(game.score, 'paire');
      },
      round: function () {
        var ws = pick(6, function (w) { return w.fr.length <= 32 && plain(w.voc).length <= 22; }, game.used);
        if (ws.length < 2) { game.used = {}; ws = pick(6, null, null); }
        if (ws.length < 2) return this.end();
        ws.forEach(function (w) { game.used[w.k] = 1; });
        game.cur = ws; game.left6 = ws.length;
        var tile = function (w, side) {
          return '<button type="button" class="rv-tile ' + side + '" data-k="' + esc(w.k) + '" data-side="' + side + '"' +
            (side === 'he' ? ' lang="he" dir="rtl"' : '') + '>' + esc(side === 'he' ? w.voc : w.fr) + '</button>';
        };
        gBody.innerHTML = '<p class="rv-help">Touche un mot hébreu puis sa traduction.</p><div class="rv-pairs">' +
          '<div class="rv-col">' + shuffle(ws).map(function (w) { return tile(w, 'he'); }).join('') + '</div>' +
          '<div class="rv-col">' + shuffle(ws).map(function (w) { return tile(w, 'fr'); }).join('') + '</div></div>';
      },
      click: function (e) {
        var du = e.target.closest('[data-dur]');
        if (du && !game.total) { game.opt = +du.dataset.dur; return this.start(game.opt); }
        var t = e.target.closest('.rv-tile');
        if (!t || t.classList.contains('ok') || game.left <= 0) return;
        var sel = game.sel;
        if (!sel || sel.dataset.side === t.dataset.side) {
          if (sel) sel.classList.remove('sel');
          if (sel === t) { game.sel = null; return; }
          t.classList.add('sel'); game.sel = t; return;
        }
        var w = game.cur.filter(function (x) { return x.k === sel.dataset.k; })[0];
        game.sel = null;
        if (sel.dataset.k === t.dataset.k) {
          sel.classList.remove('sel'); sel.classList.add('ok'); t.classList.add('ok');
          game.score++; this.clock(); stat(w, true);
          setTimeout(function () { sel.classList.add('gone'); t.classList.add('gone'); }, 220);
          if (--game.left6 <= 0) { var self = this; game.wait = setTimeout(function () { if (game && game.left > 0) self.round(); }, 380); }
        } else {
          game.err++; stat(w, false);
          [sel, t].forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('sel'); x.classList.add('bad'); setTimeout(function () { x.classList.remove('bad'); }, 420); });
        }
      },
      end: function () {
        var lbl = this.DURS.filter(function (d) { return d[0] === game.total; })[0];
        endScreen('pairs-' + game.total, game.score, plural(game.score, 'paire'),
          'En ' + (lbl ? lbl[1] : game.total + ' s') + ' · ' + (game.err ? plural(game.err, 'erreur') : 'sans erreur'), ['paire', 'paires'],
          '<button class="btn" data-rvchoose="1">Changer de durée</button>');
      }
    },

    /* ---------- Mot en désordre : 10 mots ---------- */
    scramble: {
      title: 'Mot en désordre',
      letters: function (w) { return plain(w.he).split('').filter(function (c) { return /[א-ת]/.test(c); }); },
      start: function () {
        var self = this;
        game.list = pick(10, function (w) { var n = self.letters(w).length; return n >= 3 && n <= 9; });
        if (!game.list.length) { gBody.innerHTML = '<p class="rv-help">Pas encore assez de mots pour ce jeu.</p>'; return; }
        game.i = 0; game.ok = 0;
        this.next();
      },
      next: function () {
        if (game.i >= game.list.length) return this.end();
        var w = game.list[game.i];
        game.w = w; game.target = plain(w.he).split('').filter(function (c) { return /[א-ת ]/.test(c); });
        game.pos = 0; game.err = 0; game.done = false;
        game.tiles = shuffle(this.letters(w).map(function (c, i) { return { c: c, id: i }; }));
        gProg.textContent = 'Mot ' + (game.i + 1) + ' / ' + game.list.length;
        gBar.style.width = Math.round(100 * game.i / game.list.length) + '%';
        this.draw();
      },
      skipSpaces: function () { while (game.pos < game.target.length && game.target[game.pos] === ' ') game.pos++; },
      draw: function () {
        this.skipSpaces();
        var w = game.w, slots = game.target.map(function (c, i) {
          return c === ' ' ? '<span class="rv-slot gap"></span>' : '<span class="rv-slot">' + (i < game.pos ? c : '') + '</span>';
        }).join('');
        gBody.innerHTML = '<div class="flash-card typed solo' + (game.done ? (game.err ? ' rv-is-bad' : ' rv-is-ok') : '') + '"><div class="flash-face-fr">' + esc(w.fr) + '</div>' +
          (game.done
            ? '<div class="rv-verdict ' + (game.err ? 'bad' : 'ok') + '" role="status">' +
              (game.err ? (game.gaveUp ? '✗ Voici le mot' : '≈ Trouvé, avec ' + plural(game.err, 'erreur')) : '✓ Correct, sans faute') + '</div>' +
              '<div class="rv-reveal" lang="he" dir="rtl">' + esc(w.voc) + (window.HPS ? HPS.btn(HPS.word(w.he, w.voc), 'lg') : '') + '</div>'
            : '<div class="rv-slots" dir="rtl" aria-label="Ta réponse">' + slots + '</div>' +
              '<div class="rv-letters" dir="rtl">' + game.tiles.map(function (t) {
                return '<button type="button" class="rv-letter" data-id="' + t.id + '" lang="he">' + t.c + '</button>';
              }).join('') + '</div>') +
          '</div>' +
          '<div class="flash-acts">' + (game.done
            ? '<button class="btn primary" data-rvnext="1">' + (game.i + 1 < game.list.length ? 'Mot suivant' : 'Voir le résultat') + ' <span class="flash-key">Entrée</span></button>'
            : '<button class="btn" data-rvhint="1">Indice</button><button class="btn" data-rvgive="1">Je ne sais pas</button>') + '</div>';
      },
      finish: function (gaveUp) {
        game.done = true; game.gaveUp = !!gaveUp; game.pos = game.target.length;
        if (!game.err && !gaveUp) game.ok++;
        stat(game.w, !game.err && !gaveUp);
        this.draw();
      },
      place: function (id) {
        var t = game.tiles.filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0];
        game.tiles = game.tiles.filter(function (x) { return x !== t; });
        game.pos++;
        this.skipSpaces();
        if (game.pos >= game.target.length) return this.finish(false);
        this.draw();
      },
      click: function (e) {
        if (e.target.closest('[data-rvnext]')) { game.i++; return this.next(); }
        if (game.done) return;
        if (e.target.closest('[data-rvgive]')) { game.err = Math.max(1, game.err); return this.finish(true); }
        if (e.target.closest('[data-rvhint]')) {
          var need = game.target[game.pos], h = game.tiles.filter(function (x) { return x.c === need; })[0];
          game.err++;
          if (h) this.place(h.id);
          return;
        }
        var b = e.target.closest('.rv-letter');
        if (!b) return;
        var id = +b.dataset.id, tile = game.tiles.filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0];
        if (!tile) return;
        if (tile.c === game.target[game.pos]) return this.place(id);
        game.err++;
        b.classList.add('bad'); setTimeout(function () { b.classList.remove('bad'); }, 420);
        if (game.err >= 4) { var self = this; setTimeout(function () { if (game && !game.done) self.finish(true); }, 450); }
      },
      end: function () {
        endScreen('scramble', game.ok, game.ok + ' / ' + game.list.length,
          plural(game.ok, 'mot') + ' sans faute', ['mot', 'mots']);
      }
    },

    /* ---------- Conjugaison mélangée : 12 questions ---------- */
    conj: {
      title: 'Conjugaison mélangée',
      PR: [{ fr: 'je', he: 'אֲנִי', p: 0, s: 0 }, { fr: 'tu (m.)', he: 'אַתָּה', p: 1, s: 0 }, { fr: 'il', he: 'הוּא', p: 2, s: 0 },
           { fr: 'elle', he: 'הִיא', p: 3, s: 1 }, { fr: 'nous', he: 'אֲנַחְנוּ', p: 4, s: 2 }],
      TN: [{ id: 'p', fr: 'présent' }, { id: 'a', fr: 'passé' }, { id: 'u', fr: 'futur' }],
      form: function (v, tn, pr) { return tn === 'p' ? v.p[pr.s] : v[tn][pr.p]; },
      start: function () {
        game.verbs = CONJ.filter(okVerb);
        game.n = 12; game.i = 0; game.ok = 0;
        this.next();
      },
      next: function () {
        if (game.i >= game.n) return this.end();
        var V = game.verbs, self = this, v, pr, tn, good, tries = 0;
        do {
          v = V[Math.floor(Math.random() * V.length)];
          pr = this.PR[Math.floor(Math.random() * this.PR.length)];
          tn = this.TN[Math.floor(Math.random() * this.TN.length)];
          good = this.form(v, tn.id, pr);
        } while (!good && ++tries < 30);
        var pool = [];
        var add = function (f) { if (f && f !== good && pool.indexOf(f) < 0 && plain(f) !== plain(good)) pool.push(f); };
        /* pièges : le même verbe à une autre personne ou un autre temps, puis un autre verbe au même endroit */
        var same = [];
        this.TN.forEach(function (t) { self.PR.forEach(function (p) { same.push(self.form(v, t.id, p)); }); });
        shuffle(same).forEach(add);
        var opts = pool.slice(0, 2);
        shuffle(V.filter(function (x) { return x !== v; })).forEach(function (o) {
          var f = self.form(o, tn.id, pr);
          if (opts.length < 3 && f && f !== good && opts.indexOf(f) < 0 && plain(f) !== plain(good)) opts.push(f);
        });
        pool.slice(2).forEach(function (f) { if (opts.length < 3 && opts.indexOf(f) < 0) opts.push(f); });
        game.q = { v: v, pr: pr, tn: tn, good: good, opts: shuffle(opts.concat([good])), done: false };
        gProg.textContent = 'Question ' + (game.i + 1) + ' / ' + game.n;
        gBar.style.width = Math.round(100 * game.i / game.n) + '%';
        this.draw();
      },
      draw: function (picked) {
        var q = game.q;
        var okPick = q.done && picked === q.good;
        gBody.innerHTML = '<div class="flash-card typed solo' + (q.done ? (okPick ? ' rv-is-ok' : ' rv-is-bad') : '') + '">' +
          (q.done ? '<div class="rv-verdict ' + (okPick ? 'ok' : 'bad') + '" role="status">' + (okPick ? '✓ Correct' : '✗ Faux') + '</div>' : '') +
          '<div class="rv-q"><span class="rv-chip">' + esc(q.pr.fr) + '</span><span class="rv-chip">' + q.tn.fr + '</span></div>' +
          '<div class="flash-face-he" lang="he" dir="rtl">' + esc(q.v.i) + '</div><div class="flash-ask">' + esc(q.v.f) + '</div>' +
          (q.done ? '<div class="rv-sol"><span class="rv-sol-l">' + (okPick ? 'C\'est bien' : 'La bonne réponse') + '</span><span lang="he" dir="rtl">' + esc(q.pr.he + ' ' + q.good) + (window.HPS ? HPS.btn(q.pr.he + ' ' + q.good) : '') + '</span></div>' : '') + '</div>' +
          '<div class="rv-choices">' + q.opts.map(function (f, i) {
            var cls = !q.done ? '' : f === q.good ? ' ok' : f === picked ? ' bad' : '';
            return '<button type="button" class="rv-choice' + cls + '" data-opt="' + i + '" lang="he" dir="rtl"' + (q.done ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' +
              '<span class="rv-num" aria-hidden="true">' + (i + 1) + '</span>' + esc(f) + '</button>';
          }).join('') + '</div>' +
          (q.done ? '<div class="flash-acts"><button class="btn primary" data-rvnext="1">' + (game.i + 1 < game.n ? 'Question suivante' : 'Voir le résultat') +
            ' <span class="flash-key">Entrée</span></button></div>' : '');
      },
      choose: function (i) {
        var q = game.q; if (!q || q.done) return;
        var f = q.opts[i], ok = f === q.good;
        q.done = true; if (ok) game.ok++;
        if (!storage.conj || typeof storage.conj !== 'object') storage.conj = {};
        var s = storage.conj[q.v.i] || (storage.conj[q.v.i] = { seen: 0, known: 0, again: 0, soft: 0 });
        s.seen++; if (ok) s.known++; else s.again++;
        if (window.HPG && HPG.onFlash) HPG.onFlash({ conj: true, inf: q.v.i, fr: q.v.f }, ok ? 'known' : 'again', false);
        save();
        this.draw(f);
        var nx = gBody.querySelector('[data-rvnext]'); if (nx) try { nx.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (x) {}
      },
      click: function (e) {
        if (e.target.closest('[data-rvnext]')) { stop(); game.i++; return this.next(); }
        var b = e.target.closest('[data-opt]');
        if (b) this.choose(+b.dataset.opt);
      },
      key: function (e) {
        if (/^[1-4]$/.test(e.key) && game.q && !game.q.done) { e.preventDefault(); this.choose(+e.key - 1); }
      },
      end: function () {
        endScreen('conj', game.ok, game.ok + ' / ' + game.n,
          plural(game.ok, 'bonne réponse', 'bonnes réponses'), ['bonne réponse', 'bonnes réponses']);
      }
    }
  };

  /* mise à jour : à l'ouverture de l'onglet, à la fermeture des flashcards, au retour sur la page */
  if (tabEl) tabEl.addEventListener('click', render);
  var fo = document.getElementById('flash-ov');
  if (fo && window.MutationObserver) {
    var wasOn = false;
    new MutationObserver(function () { var on = fo.classList.contains('on'); if (wasOn && !on) render(); wasOn = on; })
      .observe(fo, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class'] });
  }
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () { if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') render(); });
  try { render(); } catch (e) { console.error('Réviser', e); }
  window.HPR = { render: render, due: function () { return due().length; } };
})();
</script>
<script>
/* =====================================================================
   HPX — « Jeux de mots » : l'intrus, mot ↔ définition, phrase à compléter.
   Code commun : sert dans l'espace des élèves de Maayane ET sur le site perso de Benjamin.
   Lit le vocabulaire (VOCAB) : chaque mot est [hébreu, français, hébreu avec nikoud, définition en hébreu, phrase d'exemple].
   « L'intrus » n'a besoin que des thèmes ; les deux autres jeux se cachent quand il n'y a ni définitions ni phrases
   (sur le site de Benjamin), et s'affichent « bientôt » dans l'espace élève tant que Maayane ne les a pas préparés.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var NIK = /[\u0591-\u05BD\u05BF-\u05C7]/g;
  var VOC = (window.__HP_DATA__ && window.__HP_DATA__.VOCAB) || (typeof VOCAB !== 'undefined' ? VOCAB : {});
  var words = [];
  Object.keys(VOC).forEach(function (t) {
    (VOC[t] || []).forEach(function (e) {
      if (!e || !e[0]) return;
      words.push({ he: e[0], fr: e[1] || '', voc: e[2] || e[0], def: e[3] || '', ex: e[4] || '', theme: t });
    });
  });
  function bare(s) { return String(s || '').replace(NIK, ''); }
  function esc(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  /* repère le mot dans la phrase, avec ou sans nikoud, et le remplace par un trou */
  function blank(w) {
    if (!w.ex) return null;
    var target = bare(w.voc || w.he).trim(); if (!target) return null;
    var re = new RegExp(target.split('').map(function (ch) { return ch.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&') + '[\\u0591-\\u05C7]*'; }).join(''));
    var m = w.ex.match(re);
    if (!m) return null;
    return { before: w.ex.slice(0, m.index), after: w.ex.slice(m.index + m[0].length) };
  }
  var ready = false;
  var defs = words.filter(function (w) { return w.def; });
  var clozes = words.filter(function (w) { return blank(w); });

  /* ---------- voix ---------- */
  var voice = null;
  function pickVoice() {
    try { var v = speechSynthesis.getVoices(); voice = v.filter(function (x) { return /^he|^iw/i.test(x.lang); })[0] || null; } catch (e) {}
  }
  try { pickVoice(); speechSynthesis.addEventListener('voiceschanged', pickVoice); setTimeout(pickVoice, 1500); } catch (e) {}
  function hasVoice() { return (window.HPS && HPS.ok && HPS.ok()) || !!voice; }
  /* même moteur que le site (HPS) quand il est là : il gère les caprices de Safari et Chrome */
  function say(t, manual) {
    if (!hasVoice()) { if (manual) noVoice(); return; }
    if (window.HPS && HPS.ok && HPS.ok()) { HPS.speak(t); return; }
    try {
      speechSynthesis.cancel();
      var u = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(t); u.lang = 'he-IL'; if (voice) u.voice = voice; u.rate = 0.9;
      setTimeout(function () { speechSynthesis.speak(u); }, 60);
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  function noVoice() {
    var el = root && root.querySelector('.hpx-novoice'); if (!el) return;
    el.hidden = false;
  }

  /* ---------- interface ---------- */
  var css = '.hpx{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:9999;background:var(--bg,#f6f7fb);overflow-y:auto;font-family:var(--font-ui,system-ui,sans-serif);color:var(--ink,#222)}' +
    '.hpx-in{max-width:620px;margin:0 auto;padding:16px 16px 40px}' +
    '.hpx-top{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;margin-bottom:14px}' +
    '.hpx-top b{font:800 18px var(--font-display,inherit)}' +
    '.hpx button{font:inherit;cursor:pointer}' +
    '.hpx .x{border:1.5px solid var(--line-strong,#ccc);background:var(--paper,#fff);border-radius:999px;padding:7px 14px;font-weight:700;color:var(--ink-soft,#555)}' +
    '.hpx-modes{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:8px;margin-bottom:16px}' +
    '.hpx-q.big{font-size:36px;text-align:center}.hpx-ch.defs{grid-template-columns:1fr}.hpx-ch.defs button{font-size:17px;font-weight:600;text-align:right;padding:12px 14px}' +
    '.hpx-ch button small.tr{display:block;direction:ltr;font:600 12px var(--font-ui,sans-serif);color:var(--muted,#888);margin-top:4px}' +
    '.hpx-modes button{border:1.5px solid var(--line,#ddd);background:var(--paper,#fff);border-radius:14px;padding:12px 10px;font-weight:700;color:var(--ink-soft,#555)}' +
    '.hpx-modes button.on{border-color:var(--accent,#6d4aff);background:var(--accent-tint,#eee9ff);color:var(--accent,#6d4aff)}' +
    '.hpx-modes button:disabled{opacity:.45;cursor:default}' +
    '.hpx-card{background:var(--paper,#fff);border:1px solid var(--line,#e5e5e5);border-radius:22px;padding:22px 18px;box-shadow:var(--shadow-card,0 10px 30px -20px rgba(0,0,0,.3))}' +
    '.hpx-meta{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:13px;color:var(--muted,#888);font-weight:700;margin-bottom:12px}' +
    '.hpx-q{direction:rtl;text-align:right;font:600 24px/1.5 var(--font-he,serif);min-height:60px}' +
    '.hpx-q .gap{display:inline-block;min-width:80px;border-bottom:3px solid var(--accent,#6d4aff);margin:0 4px}' +
    '.hpx-q .gap.ok{border-color:transparent;color:var(--olive,#188a5c)}' +
    '.hpx-say{border:0;background:var(--accent-tint,#eee9ff);color:var(--accent,#6d4aff);border-radius:999px;padding:6px 12px;font-weight:700;font-size:13px;margin-top:8px}' +
    '.hpx-ch{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px;margin-top:18px}' +
    '.hpx-ch button{direction:rtl;border:1.5px solid var(--line-strong,#ccc);background:var(--paper,#fff);border-radius:16px;padding:14px 10px;font:700 21px var(--font-he,serif);color:var(--ink,#222)}' +
    '.hpx-ch button.good{border-color:var(--olive,#188a5c);background:var(--mint,#ddf6ea);color:var(--olive,#188a5c)}' +
    '.hpx-ch button.bad{border-color:var(--bad,#d93a45);background:var(--bad-tint,#ffe5e7);color:var(--bad,#d93a45)}' +
    '.hpx-fb{margin-top:16px;font-size:15px;line-height:1.5;min-height:24px}.hpx-fb .he{font-family:var(--font-he,serif);font-weight:700}' +
    '.hpx-next{display:block;width:100%;margin-top:14px;border:0;border-radius:999px;padding:14px;font-weight:800;background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--accent,#6d4aff),var(--accent-2,#b86cf2));color:var(--on-accent,#fff)}' +
    '.hpx-novoice{background:var(--peach,#ffedde);border-radius:16px;padding:12px 14px;font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.55;margin-bottom:14px}' +
    '.hpx-end{text-align:center}.hpx-end .big{font:900 54px var(--font-display,inherit);color:var(--accent,#6d4aff)}' +
    '@media(max-width:420px){.hpx-q{font-size:21px}.hpx-ch button{font-size:19px}}';
  var root = null, mode = defs.length >= 4 ? 'def' : 'cloze', serie = [], i = 0, score = 0, answered = false;

  function shuffle(a) { a = a.slice(); for (var k = a.length - 1; k > 0; k--) { var j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (k + 1)), t = a[k]; a[k] = a[j]; a[j] = t; } return a; }
  /* 3 mauvaises réponses : de préférence du même thème et de la même nature de mot */
  /* 3 mauvaises réponses : de préférence du même thème */
  function others(w, pool, n) {
    var same = shuffle(pool.filter(function (x) { return x.theme === w.theme && bare(x.voc) !== bare(w.voc); }));
    var diff = shuffle(pool.filter(function (x) { return x.theme !== w.theme && bare(x.voc) !== bare(w.voc); }));
    var seen = {}, out = [];
    same.concat(diff).forEach(function (x) { var k = bare(x.voc); if (out.length < n && !seen[k]) { seen[k] = 1; out.push(x); } });
    return out;
  }
  /* l'intrus : 3 mots d'un même thème + 1 mot d'un autre thème */
  var oddThemes = Object.keys(VOC).filter(function (t) {
    var seen = {}; (VOC[t] || []).forEach(function (e) { if (e && e[0]) seen[bare(e[2] || e[0])] = 1; });
    return Object.keys(seen).length >= 3;
  });
  var oddOk = oddThemes.length >= 2;
  var MODES = {
    def: { n: defs.length, label: 'Définition → mot', ask: 'Quel mot correspond à la définition ?' },
    rdef: { n: defs.length, label: 'Mot → définition', ask: 'Quelle est la bonne définition ?' },
    cloze: { n: clozes.length, label: 'Phrase à compléter', ask: 'Quel mot manque ?' },
    odd: { n: oddOk ? 99 : 0, label: 'L’intrus', ask: 'Quel mot n’est pas de la même famille que les autres ?' }
  };
  function can(m) { return MODES[m].n >= 4; }
  ready = can('def') || can('cloze') || can('odd');
  /* une question = { w : la bonne réponse, ch : 4 choix [{key, he}], q : html de l'énoncé, say : texte à écouter } */
  function make(m, w) {
    if (m === 'def') return { w: w, q: '<div class="hpx-q">' + esc(w.def) + '</div>', say: w.def, sayLbl: 'Écouter la définition',
      ch: shuffle(others(w, words, 3).concat([w])).map(function (c) { return { key: bare(c.voc), he: c.voc }; }), good: bare(w.voc) };
    if (m === 'rdef') return { w: w, q: '<div class="hpx-q big" lang="he">' + esc(w.voc) + '</div>', say: w.voc, sayLbl: 'Écouter le mot', small: true,
      ch: shuffle(others(w, defs, 3).concat([w])).map(function (c) { return { key: bare(c.voc), he: c.def }; }), good: bare(w.voc) };
    if (m === 'cloze') { var b = blank(w); return { w: w, gap: true, q: '<div class="hpx-q">' + esc(b.before) + '<span class="gap" id="hpx-gap">&nbsp;</span>' + esc(b.after) + '</div>', say: w.ex, sayLbl: 'Écouter la phrase',
      ch: shuffle(others(w, words, 3).concat([w])).map(function (c) { return { key: bare(c.voc), he: c.voc }; }), good: bare(w.voc) }; }
    /* intrus */
    var th = shuffle(oddThemes), A = th[0], B = th[1];
    var famA = shuffle(words.filter(function (x) { return x.theme === A; })), three = [], seen = {};
    famA.forEach(function (x) { var k = bare(x.voc); if (three.length < 3 && !seen[k]) { seen[k] = 1; three.push(x); } });
    var odd = shuffle(words.filter(function (x) { return x.theme === B && !seen[bare(x.voc)]; }))[0];
    return { w: odd, odd: true, fam: A, q: '', ch: shuffle(three.concat([odd])).map(function (c) { return { key: bare(c.voc), he: c.voc, fr: c.fr }; }), good: bare(odd.voc) };
  }
  function start(m) {
    if (!can(m)) return;
    mode = m;
    var pool = m === 'cloze' ? clozes : m === 'odd' ? null : defs;
    serie = pool ? shuffle(pool).slice(0, Math.min(10, pool.length)).map(function (w) { return make(m, w); })
      : Array.apply(null, Array(10)).map(function () { return make('odd'); });
    i = 0; score = 0;
    render();
  }
  function render() {
    if (!root) return;
    var body;
    if (i >= serie.length) {
      body = '<div class="hpx-card hpx-end"><div class="big">' + score + ' / ' + serie.length + '</div>' +
        '<p>' + (score === serie.length ? 'Parfait ! 🎉' : score >= serie.length * 0.7 ? 'Très bien ! 💪' : 'Continue, ça vient ! 🌱') + '</p>' +
        '<button class="hpx-next" data-again>Rejouer</button></div>';
    } else {
      var Q = serie[i];
      body = '<div class="hpx-card"><div class="hpx-meta"><span>' + MODES[mode].ask + '</span><span>' + (i + 1) + ' / ' + serie.length + ' · ✓ ' + score + '</span></div>' +
        Q.q + (Q.say ? '<button class="hpx-say" data-hpxsay="' + esc(Q.say) + '">🔊 ' + Q.sayLbl + '</button>' : '') +
        '<div class="hpx-ch' + (Q.small ? ' defs' : '') + (Q.odd ? ' odd' : '') + '">' + Q.ch.map(function (c) { return '<button data-c="' + esc(c.key) + '" lang="he">' + esc(c.he) + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="hpx-fb" id="hpx-fb"></div></div>';
    }
    root.querySelector('.hpx-body').innerHTML = body;
    root.querySelectorAll('.hpx-modes button').forEach(function (b) { b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.m === mode); });
    answered = false;
  }
  function answer(btn) {
    if (answered) return; answered = true;
    var Q = serie[i], w = Q.w, ok = btn.dataset.c === Q.good;
    if (ok) score++;
    try { if (typeof updateWordStat === 'function' && w && w.he) updateWordStat({ he: w.he, fr: w.fr }, ok ? 'correct' : 'wrong'); } catch (e) {}
    root.querySelectorAll('.hpx-ch button').forEach(function (b, k) {
      if (b.dataset.c === Q.good) b.classList.add('good'); else if (b === btn) b.classList.add('bad');
      if (Q.odd) b.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<small class="tr">' + esc(Q.ch[k].fr) + '</small>');
      b.disabled = true;
    });
    var gap = root.querySelector('#hpx-gap'); if (gap) { gap.textContent = w.voc; gap.classList.add('ok'); }
    var fb = Q.odd
      ? (ok ? '✅ Bravo ! ' : '❌ ') + 'L’intrus : <span class="he" lang="he" dir="rtl">' + esc(w.voc) + '</span> (' + esc(w.fr) + ', thème « ' + esc(w.theme) + ' »). Les autres : « ' + esc(Q.fam) + ' ».'
      : (ok ? '✅ Bravo ! ' : '❌ C’était ') + '<span class="he" lang="he" dir="rtl">' + esc(w.voc) + '</span> = ' + esc(w.fr) + (mode === 'rdef' ? '<br><span class="he" lang="he" dir="rtl">' + esc(w.def) + '</span>' : '');
    root.querySelector('#hpx-fb').innerHTML = fb + '<button class="hpx-next" data-next>' + (i + 1 < serie.length ? 'Suivant →' : 'Voir mon score') + '</button>';
    say(w.voc);
  }
  function open(m) {
    if (root || !ready) return;
    mode = m && can(m) ? m : ['def', 'cloze', 'odd'].filter(can)[0];
    if (!document.getElementById('hpx-css')) { var s = document.createElement('style'); s.id = 'hpx-css'; s.textContent = css; document.head.appendChild(s); }
    root = document.createElement('div'); root.className = 'hpx';
    root.innerHTML = '<div class="hpx-in"><div class="hpx-top"><b>🧩 Jeux de mots</b><button class="x" data-close>✕ Fermer</button></div>' +
      '<div class="hpx-modes">' + Object.keys(MODES).map(function (k) { return '<button data-m="' + k + '"' + (can(k) ? '' : ' disabled') + '>' + MODES[k].label + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="hpx-novoice" hidden><b>🔇 Pas de voix hébraïque sur cet appareil</b>, le son ne peut pas fonctionner. Pour l’activer :<br>' +
      '• iPhone / iPad : Réglages › Accessibilité › Contenu énoncé › Voix › Hébreu (télécharger)<br>' +
      '• Android : Paramètres › Synthèse vocale (Google) › Installer les données vocales › Hébreu<br>' +
      '• Ordinateur : ouvre le site dans Chrome ou Edge.</div>' +
      '<div class="hpx-body"></div></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(root);
    root.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var t = e.target.closest('button'); if (!t) return;
      if (t.hasAttribute('data-close')) close();
      else if (t.dataset.m) start(t.dataset.m);
      else if (t.dataset.c != null) answer(t);
      else if (t.hasAttribute('data-next')) { i++; render(); }
      else if (t.hasAttribute('data-again')) start(mode);
      else if (t.dataset.hpxsay) say(t.dataset.hpxsay, true);
    });
    start(mode);
  }
  function close() { if (root) { root.remove(); root = null; } try { speechSynthesis.cancel(); } catch (e) {} }
  window.HPX = { open: function () { open(); }, close: close };
  /* cartes dans « Jeux courts » (onglet Réviser), à côté des jeux du site */
  function card(m, ic, title, txt, n, soon) {
    var ok = n >= 4;
    if (!ok && !window.__HP_STUDENT__ && !window.__HP_DATA__) return '';
    return '<button type="button" class="rv-game" data-hpx="' + m + '"' + (ok ? '' : ' disabled style="opacity:.55;cursor:default"') + '><span class="rv-ic" aria-hidden="true">' + ic + '</span><b>' + title + '</b>' +
      '<span>' + txt + '</span><small>' + (ok ? '🔊 Avec audio' + (n < 99 ? ' · ' + n + ' mots' : '') : soon) + '</small></button>';
  }
  function addCards() {
    document.querySelectorAll('.rv-games').forEach(function (g) {
      if (g.querySelector('[data-hpx]')) return;
      var soon = 'Bientôt : ta professeure les prépare';
      g.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',
        card('odd', '🕵️', 'L’intrus', '4 mots, 3 de la même famille : trouve celui qui n’a rien à faire là. 10 questions.', MODES.odd.n, 'Il faut au moins deux thèmes') +
        card('def', '🧩', 'Mot ↔ définition', 'Une définition simple en hébreu → le mot, ou le mot → sa définition. 10 questions.', defs.length, soon) +
        card('cloze', '✍️', 'Phrase à compléter', 'Une phrase en hébreu, un mot manque : lequel ? 10 questions.', clozes.length, soon));
    });
  }
  function init() {
    var b = document.getElementById('hp-px-btn'); if (b && ready) b.hidden = false;
    addCards();
    /* l'onglet Réviser se redessine : on remet les cartes à chaque fois */
    new MutationObserver(function () { addCards(); }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    document.addEventListener('click', function (e) { var c = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-hpx]'); if (c && !c.disabled) open(c.dataset.hpx); });
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init); else init();
})();
</script>
</body></html>
